# >>> UA-ART-ISSUE84-PERMANENT-SPEC-V1
from ua_spec_permanent import ensure_html as _ua84_ensure_html
from ua_spec_permanent import card_uid_from_path as _ua84_uid_from_path
from ua_spec_permanent import write_lock as _ua84_write_lock

def _ua84_require_uid(value):
    candidate = _ua84_uid_from_path(str(value) + ".html")
    if candidate is None:
        raise ValueError("UA84_CARD_UID_REQUIRED")
    return candidate

def _ua84_model_uid(model):
    # Existing generators use nomer(model); its result is validated before use.
    value = None
    if hasattr(model, "get"):
        value = model.get("auto_number")
    if not value:
        value = nomer(model)
    return _ua84_require_uid(value)

_ua84_previous_zapisat_atomarno = _zapisat_atomarno
def _zapisat_atomarno(put, tekst):
    uid = _ua84_uid_from_path(put)
    if uid is not None:
        with _ua84_write_lock():
            tekst = _ua84_ensure_html(tekst, uid)
            return _ua84_previous_zapisat_atomarno(put, tekst)
    return _ua84_previous_zapisat_atomarno(put, tekst)
# <<< UA-ART-ISSUE84-PERMANENT-SPEC-V1


# ===== publish_transaction_guard.py =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Transactional publication guard for UA ART.

The active legacy publisher expects a diagnostic target to exist before it
can render a new primary page.  This guard stages the diagnostic page first,
backs up the complete bounded publication bundle, invokes the approved master
publisher, rebuilds both catalogs, verifies the result and rolls everything
back on any failure.

Runtime LLM use: none.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import contextlib
import fcntl
import hashlib
import io
import json
import os
import pathlib
import re
import shutil
import sqlite3
import tempfile
import time
import uuid
from typing import Any, Callable, Iterable


CONTRACT_ID = "UA-0012-UA-0013-PUBLISH-TRANSACTION-001-V1.0"
ROOT = pathlib.Path(os.environ.get("UA_ART_ROOT", "/home/Carix")).resolve()
VIDEO = ROOT / "video"
SITE = ROOT / "site"
DB = ROOT / "crm.db"
LOCK = ROOT / ".ua_art_publish_transaction.lock"
BACKUPS = ROOT / "rezerv_publikacii" / "TASK083"
LOG = ROOT / "publish_log.txt"
ROOTS = (VIDEO, SITE)
ID_RE = re.compile(r"^UA-[0-9]{4,}$")
MAX_FILE = 32 * 1024 * 1024
WAIT_SECONDS = 90


class PublishError(RuntimeError):
    pass


def _sha(data: bytes) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()


def _read(path: pathlib.Path) -> bytes:
    data = path.read_bytes()
    if len(data) > MAX_FILE:
        raise PublishError("FILE_TOO_LARGE:" + str(path))
    return data


def _atomic(path: pathlib.Path, data: bytes, mode: int | None = None) -> None:
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    handle = tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(
        dir=path.parent, prefix="." + path.name + ".", suffix=".task083.tmp", delete=False
    )
    temp = pathlib.Path(handle.name)
    try:
        with handle:
            handle.write(data)
            handle.flush()
            os.fsync(handle.fileno())
        if mode is not None:
            os.chmod(temp, mode)
        os.replace(temp, path)
    finally:
        temp.unlink(missing_ok=True)


def _append_log(message: str) -> None:
    try:
        with io.open(LOG, "a", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
            handle.write("%s  TASK083 %s\n" % (time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"), message))
    except Exception:
        pass


def _code(value: Any) -> str:
    result = str(value or "").strip().upper()
    if not ID_RE.fullmatch(result):
        raise PublishError("INVALID_AUTO_NUMBER:" + result[:40])
    return result


def _stage(row):
    from ua_delivery_status import stage_number
    return stage_number(row.get("status"))


def _category(stage: int) -> str:
    return {1: "korea", 2: "more", 3: "gruzia", 4: "kiev"}[stage]


def _rows(*, include_hidden=False) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], str]:
    from ua_delivery_status import stage_number
    connection = sqlite3.connect("file:%s?mode=ro" % DB, uri=True, timeout=30)
    connection.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
    try:
        connection.execute("PRAGMA query_only=ON")
        quick = str(connection.execute("PRAGMA quick_check").fetchone()[0])
        rows = [dict(row) for row in connection.execute(
            "SELECT * FROM cars WHERE published=1 ORDER BY auto_number, id"
        ).fetchall()]
    finally:
        connection.close()
    if quick != "ok":
        raise PublishError("CRM_QUICK_CHECK:" + quick)
    normalized = json.dumps(rows, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, default=str).encode()
    visible = rows if include_hidden else [row for row in rows if stage_number(row.get("status"))]
    return visible, _sha(normalized)


def _row_map(*, include_hidden=False) -> tuple[dict[str, dict[str, Any]], str]:
    rows, digest = _rows(include_hidden=include_hidden)
    return {str(row.get("auto_number") or "").upper(): row for row in rows}, digest


def _protected_pages(codes: set[str]) -> dict[str, str]:
    result: dict[str, str] = {}
    for root in ROOTS:
        if not root.is_dir():
            continue
        for path in sorted(root.glob("UA-*.html")):
            if any(path.name.startswith(code) for code in codes):
                continue
            result[str(path)] = _sha(_read(path))
    return result


def _matching_paths(codes: set[str]) -> set[pathlib.Path]:
    paths: set[pathlib.Path] = {root / "katalog.html" for root in ROOTS}
    for code in codes:
        for root in ROOTS:
            paths.add(root / (code + ".html"))
            paths.add(root / (code + "-diag.html"))
            if root.is_dir():
                paths.update(path for path in root.glob(code + "*") if path.is_file())
            for folder in (root / "foto" / code, root / "diag" / code):
                if folder.is_dir():
                    paths.update(path for path in folder.rglob("*") if path.is_file())
        archive = ROOT / "archive" / "video_dubli"
        if archive.is_dir():
            paths.update(path for path in archive.glob(code + "*") if path.is_file())
    return paths


class Snapshot:
    """Persistent bounded backup with exact rollback semantics."""

    def __init__(self, codes: Iterable[str]):
        self.codes = {_code(value) for value in codes}
        stamp = time.strftime("%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ", time.gmtime())
        self.root = BACKUPS / (stamp + "-" + uuid.uuid4().hex[:12])
        self.root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
        self.before_paths = _matching_paths(self.codes)
        self.present = {path for path in self.before_paths if path.is_file()}
        manifest: dict[str, Any] = {}
        for path in sorted(self.before_paths):
            item: dict[str, Any] = {"exists": path in self.present, "path": str(path)}
            if path in self.present:
                data = _read(path)
                relative = path.relative_to(ROOT)
                target = self.root / "files" / relative
                _atomic(target, data, path.stat().st_mode & 0o777)
                item.update({"sha256": _sha(data), "mode": path.stat().st_mode & 0o777})
            manifest[str(path)] = item
        _atomic(
            self.root / "manifest.json",
            (json.dumps(manifest, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, sort_keys=True) + "\n").encode(),
        )

    def restore(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        current = _matching_paths(self.codes)
        removed: list[str] = []
        restored: list[str] = []
        for path in sorted(current - self.present, reverse=True):
            if path.is_file():
                path.unlink()
                removed.append(str(path))
        manifest = json.loads((self.root / "manifest.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        for path in sorted(self.present):
            item = manifest[str(path)]
            data = _read(self.root / "files" / path.relative_to(ROOT))
            if not path.is_file() or _read(path) != data:
                _atomic(path, data, int(item.get("mode") or 0o644))
                restored.append(str(path))
        mismatches = []
        for path in self.present:
            expected = manifest[str(path)]["sha256"]
            if not path.is_file() or _sha(_read(path)) != expected:
                mismatches.append(str(path))
        if mismatches:
            raise PublishError("ROLLBACK_READBACK_MISMATCH:" + ",".join(mismatches))
        return {"backup_root": str(self.root), "removed": removed, "restored": restored}


@contextlib.contextmanager
def _exclusive_lock():
    LOCK.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    handle = open(LOCK, "a+")
    deadline = time.monotonic() + WAIT_SECONDS
    try:
        while True:
            try:
                fcntl.flock(handle.fileno(), fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
                break
            except BlockingIOError:
                if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                    raise PublishError("PUBLISH_LOCK_TIMEOUT")
                time.sleep(0.25)
        yield
    finally:
        try:
            fcntl.flock(handle.fileno(), fcntl.LOCK_UN)
        finally:
            handle.close()


def _render_diag(code: str) -> bytes:
    import stranica as page

    row = None
    for item in page.mashiny():
        if str(page.nomer(item)).strip().upper() == code:
            row = item
            break
    if row is None:
        raise PublishError("PUBLISHED_CARD_NOT_FOUND:" + code)
    value = page.sobrat_diagnostiku(row)
    if isinstance(value, bytes):
        data = value
        text = value.decode("utf-8", "replace")
    else:
        text = str(value or "")
        data = text.encode("utf-8")
    if len(data) < 300 or code not in text or "</html>" not in text.lower():
        raise PublishError("DIAGNOSTIC_PLACEHOLDER_INVALID:" + code)
    if re.search(r"UA-[0-9]{4,}", text) and any(
        other != code for other in set(re.findall(r"UA-[0-9]{4,}", text, re.I))
    ):
        raise PublishError("DIAGNOSTIC_FOREIGN_IDENTIFIER:" + code)
    # >>> UA-ART-SITE-GE-PERSISTENCE-V1: TRANSACTION_DIAG_OUTPUT
    from site_ge_inject import inject_html as _ua_site_ge_inject_html
    text = _ua_site_ge_inject_html(text)
    data = text.encode("utf-8")
    # <<< UA-ART-SITE-GE-PERSISTENCE-V1: TRANSACTION_DIAG_OUTPUT
    return data


def _stage_diags(codes: list[str]) -> dict[str, str]:
    result: dict[str, str] = {}
    for code in codes:
        data = _render_diag(code)
        for root in ROOTS:
            if not root.is_dir():
                raise PublishError("SITE_ROOT_MISSING:" + str(root))
            path = root / (code + "-diag.html")
            _atomic(path, data, path.stat().st_mode & 0o777 if path.exists() else 0o644)
            if _read(path) != data:
                raise PublishError("DIAGNOSTIC_READBACK_MISMATCH:" + str(path))
        result[code] = _sha(data)
    return result


def _href_counts(source: str) -> dict[str, int]:
    pattern = re.compile(
        r"href\s*=\s*['\"](?:https?://[^'\"]+)?(?:[^'\"]*/)?"
        r"(UA-[0-9]{4,})\.html(?:\?[^'\"]*)?['\"]",
        re.I,
    )
    counts: dict[str, int] = {}
    for value in pattern.findall(source):
        code = value.upper()
        counts[code] = counts.get(code, 0) + 1
    return counts


def _catalog_card_window(source: str, code: str) -> str:
    match = re.search(
        r"href\s*=\s*['\"][^'\"]*" + re.escape(code) + r"\.html(?:\?[^'\"]*)?['\"]",
        source,
        re.I,
    )
    if not match:
        return ""
    start = max(0, match.start() - 1200)
    end = min(len(source), match.end() + 14000)
    return source[start:end]


def _validate_catalog(source: str, rows: dict[str, dict[str, Any]]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if len(source.encode("utf-8")) < 5000 or "</html>" not in source.lower():
        raise PublishError("CATALOG_HTML_INVALID")
    counts = _href_counts(source)
    expected = set(rows)
    wrong = {code: counts.get(code, 0) for code in sorted(expected) if counts.get(code, 0) != 1}
    unexpected = sorted(set(counts) - expected)
    if wrong:
        raise PublishError("CATALOG_CARD_COUNT:" + json.dumps(wrong, sort_keys=True))
    if unexpected:
        raise PublishError("CATALOG_UNEXPECTED_IDS:" + ",".join(unexpected))
    cards: dict[str, Any] = {}
    for code, row in rows.items():
        stage = _stage(row)
        category = _category(stage)
        window = _catalog_card_window(source, code)
        tokens = (
            'data-ua-stage="%d"' % stage,
            "data-ua-stage='%d'" % stage,
            'data-ua-stage-tile="%d"' % stage,
            "data-ua-stage-tile='%d'" % stage,
            'data-stage="%d"' % stage,
            "data-stage='%d'" % stage,
        )
        if not window or not any(token in window for token in tokens):
            raise PublishError("CATALOG_STAGE_MISMATCH:%s:%d" % (code, stage))
        cards[code] = {
            "href_count": 1,
            "stage": stage,
            "category": category,
            "category_token": bool(re.search(
                r"(?:data-(?:category|filter)|[?&](?:f|etap|stage)=)['\"]?" + re.escape(category),
                window,
                re.I,
            )),
        }
    return {"count": len(expected), "cards": cards, "ids": sorted(expected)}


def _validate_primary(code: str, row: dict[str, Any], source: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if len(source.encode("utf-8")) < 5000 or code not in source or "</html>" not in source.lower():
        raise PublishError("PRIMARY_HTML_INVALID:" + code)
    links = re.findall(
        r"href\s*=\s*['\"](?:[^'\"]*/)?" + re.escape(code) + r"-diag\.html(?:\?[^'\"]*)?['\"]",
        source,
        re.I,
    )
    if len(links) != 1:
        raise PublishError("PRIMARY_DIAGNOSTIC_LINK_COUNT:%s:%d" % (code, len(links)))
    foreign = {
        value.upper() for value in re.findall(r"UA-[0-9]{4,}", source, re.I)
        if value.upper() != code
    }
    if foreign:
        raise PublishError("PRIMARY_FOREIGN_IDENTIFIER:%s:%s" % (code, ",".join(sorted(foreign))))
    stage = _stage(row)
    stage_tokens = (
        'data-ua-stage-current="%d"' % stage,
        "data-ua-stage-current='%d'" % stage,
        'data-ua-stage="%d"' % stage,
        "data-ua-stage='%d'" % stage,
    )
    if not any(token in source for token in stage_tokens):
        raise PublishError("PRIMARY_STAGE_MISMATCH:%s:%d" % (code, stage))
    return {"bytes": len(source.encode("utf-8")), "diag_links": 1, "stage": stage}


def _build_catalog() -> tuple[str, list[dict[str, Any]]]:
    import publikaciya as publisher

    if not hasattr(publisher, "_ua9_sobrat_katalog"):
        raise PublishError("CATALOG_MASTER_MISSING")
    value, rows = publisher._ua9_sobrat_katalog()
    if isinstance(value, bytes):
        value = value.decode("utf-8", "replace")
    return str(value or ""), list(rows or [])


def _install_catalog(source: str, target: str | None) -> dict[str, Any]:
    row_map, _ = _row_map()
    before = _validate_catalog(source, row_map)
    try:
        import catalog_stage_guard_core as stage_guard
    except ModuleNotFoundError as exc:
        raise PublishError("CATALOG_STAGE_GUARD_MISSING") from exc

    photos: dict[str, str] = {}
    for code in sorted(row_map):
        primary_path = VIDEO / (code + ".html")
        if not primary_path.is_file():
            raise PublishError("CATALOG_PRIMARY_NOT_READY:" + code)
        primary = _read(primary_path).decode("utf-8", "replace")
        photos[code] = stage_guard.extract_main_photo(
            primary, "https://www.uaart.com.ua/video/%s.html" % code, code
        )
        if not photos[code]:
            raise PublishError("CATALOG_MAIN_PHOTO_NOT_READY:" + code)

    try:
        normalized = stage_guard.enforce_catalog(source, list(row_map.values()), photos)
        stage_audit = stage_guard.audit_catalog(normalized, list(row_map.values()))
    except Exception as exc:
        raise PublishError("CATALOG_STAGE_GUARD_FAIL:" + str(exc)) from exc
    if not isinstance(stage_audit, dict) or stage_audit.get("status") != "PASS":
        raise PublishError("CATALOG_STAGE_AUDIT_FAIL")
    data = normalized.encode("utf-8")
    for root in ROOTS:
        path = root / "katalog.html"
        _atomic(path, data, path.stat().st_mode & 0o777 if path.exists() else 0o644)
        if _read(path) != data:
            raise PublishError("CATALOG_READBACK_MISMATCH:" + str(path))

    after: dict[str, Any] = {}
    for root in ROOTS:
        path = root / "katalog.html"
        after[str(path)] = {
            "sha256": _sha(_read(path)),
            "audit": _validate_catalog(_read(path).decode("utf-8", "replace"), row_map),
        }
    return {
        "candidate": before,
        "stage_guard": stage_audit,
        "target": target,
        "installed": after,
    }


def _verify_bundle(codes: list[str]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    rows, digest = _row_map()
    targets: dict[str, Any] = {}
    for code in codes:
        row = rows.get(code)
        if row is None:
            raise PublishError("TARGET_NOT_PUBLISHED:" + code)
        files: dict[str, Any] = {}
        for root in ROOTS:
            primary_path = root / (code + ".html")
            diag_path = root / (code + "-diag.html")
            if not primary_path.is_file() or not diag_path.is_file():
                raise PublishError("TARGET_FILE_MISSING:%s:%s" % (code, root))
            primary_text = _read(primary_path).decode("utf-8", "replace")
            diag_text = _read(diag_path).decode("utf-8", "replace")
            if code not in diag_text or "</html>" not in diag_text.lower():
                raise PublishError("DIAGNOSTIC_READBACK_INVALID:%s:%s" % (code, root))
            files[str(root)] = {
                "primary_sha256": _sha(_read(primary_path)),
                "diag_sha256": _sha(_read(diag_path)),
                "primary": _validate_primary(code, row, primary_text),
            }
        targets[code] = {
            "status": row.get("status"),
            "stage": _stage(row),
            "category": _category(_stage(row)),
            "files": files,
        }
    catalogs: dict[str, Any] = {}
    for root in ROOTS:
        path = root / "katalog.html"
        catalogs[str(path)] = _validate_catalog(
            _read(path).decode("utf-8", "replace"), rows
        )
    return {"targets": targets, "catalogs": catalogs, "published_rows_sha256": digest}


def _call_base(base_publish: Callable[..., Any], code: str, proba: bool) -> tuple[bool, str]:
    value = base_publish(code, proba=proba)
    if not isinstance(value, (tuple, list)) or len(value) < 2:
        raise PublishError("PUBLISHER_RESULT_INVALID:" + code)
    return value[0] is True, str(value[1])


def _publish_locked(
    base_publish: Callable[..., Any], codes: list[str], proba: bool = False
) -> tuple[bool, str, dict[str, Any]]:
    codes = [_code(value) for value in codes]
    if not codes or len(codes) != len(set(codes)):
        raise PublishError("TARGET_SET_INVALID")
    snapshot = Snapshot(codes)
    before_rows, before_db = _row_map()
    missing = [code for code in codes if code not in before_rows]
    if missing:
        raise PublishError("TARGETS_NOT_PUBLISHED:" + ",".join(missing))
    protected = _protected_pages(set(codes))
    evidence: dict[str, Any] = {
        "contract_id": CONTRACT_ID,
        "codes": codes,
        "mode": "PROBE" if proba else "INSTALL",
        "backup_root": str(snapshot.root),
        "published_rows_sha256_before": before_db,
        "staged_diagnostics": {},
        "base_results": {},
        "catalog": None,
        "verification": None,
        "rollback": None,
    }
    try:
        evidence["staged_diagnostics"] = _stage_diags(codes)
        for code in codes:
            ok, message = _call_base(base_publish, code, proba)
            evidence["base_results"][code] = {"ok": ok, "message": message[:2000]}
            if not ok:
                raise PublishError("BASE_PUBLISH_FAILED:%s:%s" % (code, message[:500]))

        catalog_source, catalog_rows = _build_catalog()
        generated_ids = {str(row.get("auto_number") or "").upper() for row in catalog_rows}
        if generated_ids != set(before_rows):
            raise PublishError("CATALOG_ROW_SET_MISMATCH")
        _validate_catalog(catalog_source, before_rows)

        if proba:
            evidence["rollback"] = snapshot.restore()
            evidence["status"] = "PASS"
            return True, "Проба публикации прошла; production-файлы не изменены.", evidence

        evidence["catalog"] = _install_catalog(catalog_source, codes[-1])
        after_rows, after_db = _row_map()
        if before_db != after_db or set(before_rows) != set(after_rows):
            raise PublishError("CRM_ROWS_CHANGED_DURING_PUBLICATION")
        if _protected_pages(set(codes)) != protected:
            raise PublishError("PROTECTED_PAGE_CHANGED")
        evidence["verification"] = _verify_bundle(codes)
        evidence["published_rows_sha256_after"] = after_db
        evidence["status"] = "PASS"
        _append_log("PASS %s backup=%s" % (",".join(codes), snapshot.root))
        return True, (
            "Страницы, диагностика и оба каталога опубликованы одним проверенным пакетом: "
            + ", ".join(codes)
        ), evidence
    except Exception as exc:
        try:
            evidence["rollback"] = snapshot.restore()
        except Exception as rollback_exc:
            evidence["rollback_error"] = type(rollback_exc).__name__ + ":" + str(rollback_exc)
            _append_log("CRITICAL ROLLBACK FAIL %s %s" % (",".join(codes), rollback_exc))
            raise
        evidence["status"] = "FAIL"
        evidence["error"] = type(exc).__name__ + ":" + str(exc)
        _append_log("FAIL %s %s rollback=PASS" % (",".join(codes), evidence["error"]))
        return False, "Публикация отменена: %s. Выполнен полный откат." % str(exc), evidence


def publish_batch(
    base_publish: Callable[..., Any], codes: Iterable[str], proba: bool = False
) -> tuple[bool, str, dict[str, Any]]:
    with _exclusive_lock():
        return _publish_locked(base_publish, list(codes), proba=proba)


def publish_one(
    base_publish: Callable[..., Any], code: str, proba: bool = False
) -> tuple[bool, str]:
    ok, message, _ = publish_batch(base_publish, [code], proba=proba)
    return ok, message


def rebuild_catalog() -> tuple[bool, str]:
    with _exclusive_lock():
        snapshot = Snapshot([])
        before_rows, before_db = _row_map()
        protected = _protected_pages(set())
        try:
            source, generated_rows = _build_catalog()
            generated_ids = {str(row.get("auto_number") or "").upper() for row in generated_rows}
            if generated_ids != set(before_rows):
                raise PublishError("CATALOG_ROW_SET_MISMATCH")
            _install_catalog(source, None)
            _, after_db = _row_map()
            if after_db != before_db:
                raise PublishError("CRM_ROWS_CHANGED_DURING_CATALOG_REBUILD")
            if _protected_pages(set()) != protected:
                raise PublishError("PROTECTED_PAGE_CHANGED")
            return True, "Каталог обновлён."
        except Exception as exc:
            snapshot.restore()
            return False, "Каталог не обновлён: %s. Выполнен откат." % str(exc)


def verify_bundle(codes: Iterable[str]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Read-only verification used by the deployment postcheck."""
    normalized = [_code(value) for value in codes]
    with _exclusive_lock():
        return _verify_bundle(normalized)


def rollback_backup(backup_root: str, codes: Iterable[str]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Restore a previously recorded publication snapshot."""
    root = pathlib.Path(backup_root).resolve()
    if root == BACKUPS or BACKUPS not in root.parents:
        raise PublishError("ROLLBACK_BACKUP_OUTSIDE_TASK083")
    manifest_path = root / "manifest.json"
    if not manifest_path.is_file():
        raise PublishError("ROLLBACK_MANIFEST_MISSING")
    manifest = json.loads(manifest_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    instance = Snapshot.__new__(Snapshot)
    instance.codes = {_code(value) for value in codes}
    instance.root = root
    instance.before_paths = {pathlib.Path(value) for value in manifest}
    instance.present = {
        pathlib.Path(value) for value, item in manifest.items() if item.get("exists")
    }
    with _exclusive_lock():
        return instance.restore()


__all__ = [
    "CONTRACT_ID",
    "PublishError",
    "publish_batch",
    "publish_one",
    "rebuild_catalog",
    "verify_bundle",
    "rollback_backup",
]
# CATALOG-DESIGN-STAGE-RESTORE-090-V1.0:PUBLISH:START
def _validate_catalog(source, rows):
    import catalog_design_guard as _ua090_design
    _golden = _ua090_design.GOLDEN_PATH.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    _audit = _ua090_design.audit_catalog(source, rows.values(), _golden)
    if _audit.get("status") != "PASS":
        raise PublishError("TASK090_CATALOG_CONTRACT:" + ",".join(_audit.get("errors") or []))
    return _audit


def _install_catalog(source, target):
    import catalog_design_guard as _ua090_design
    _rows_map, _rows_digest = _row_map()
    _photos = {}
    for _identifier in sorted(_rows_map):
        _primary_path = VIDEO / (_identifier + ".html")
        if not _primary_path.is_file():
            raise PublishError("TASK090_PRIMARY_NOT_READY:" + _identifier)
        _primary = _read(_primary_path).decode("utf-8", "replace")
        _photos[_identifier] = _ua090_design.extract_main_photo(_primary, _identifier)

    _normalized = _ua090_design.build_from_golden_path(_rows_map.values(), _photos)
    # >>> UA-ART-SITE-GE-PERSISTENCE-V1: TRANSACTION_CATALOG_OUTPUT
    from site_ge_inject import inject_html as _ua_site_ge_inject_html
    _normalized = _ua_site_ge_inject_html(_normalized)
    # <<< UA-ART-SITE-GE-PERSISTENCE-V1: TRANSACTION_CATALOG_OUTPUT
    _before = _validate_catalog(_normalized, _rows_map)
    _data = _normalized.encode("utf-8")
    for _root in ROOTS:
        _path = _root / "katalog.html"
        _atomic(_path, _data, _path.stat().st_mode & 0o777 if _path.exists() else 0o644)
        if _read(_path) != _data:
            raise PublishError("TASK090_CATALOG_READBACK:" + str(_path))

    _installed = {}
    for _root in ROOTS:
        _path = _root / "katalog.html"
        _text = _read(_path).decode("utf-8", "replace")
        _installed[str(_path)] = {
            "sha256": _sha(_read(_path)),
            "audit": _validate_catalog(_text, _rows_map),
        }
    return {
        "candidate": _before,
        "design_guard": _before,
        "target": target,
        "published_rows_sha256": _rows_digest,
        "installed": _installed,
    }
# CATALOG-DESIGN-STAGE-RESTORE-090-V1.0:PUBLISH:END

# >>> UA099 BOUNDED GZIP SNAPSHOT V1
# The publisher writes card HTML, diagnostic HTML and catalogs only.  Media
# directories are operator-owned and are deliberately excluded from this
# transaction snapshot.  A separate TASK099 outer backup protects every
# public target before this guard is installed.
import gzip as _ua099_gzip

def _matching_paths(codes):
    normalized = {_code(value) for value in codes}
    paths = {root / "katalog.html" for root in ROOTS}
    for code in normalized:
        for root in ROOTS:
            paths.add(root / (code + ".html"))
            paths.add(root / (code + "-diag.html"))
            if root.is_dir():
                paths.update(path for path in root.glob(code + "*.html") if path.is_file())
    return paths


class Snapshot:
    """Exact rollback snapshot, bounded to publisher-written HTML and gzip stored."""

    def __init__(self, codes):
        self.codes = {_code(value) for value in codes}
        stamp = time.strftime("%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ", time.gmtime())
        self.root = BACKUPS / (stamp + "-" + uuid.uuid4().hex[:12])
        self.root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
        self.before_paths = _matching_paths(self.codes)
        self.present = {path for path in self.before_paths if path.is_file()}
        manifest = {}
        for path in sorted(self.before_paths):
            item = {"exists": path in self.present, "path": str(path)}
            if path in self.present:
                data = _read(path)
                relative = path.relative_to(ROOT)
                stored_relative = pathlib.Path("files") / (str(relative) + ".gz")
                target = self.root / stored_relative
                packed = _ua099_gzip.compress(data, compresslevel=9, mtime=0)
                _atomic(target, packed, 0o600)
                item.update({
                    "sha256": _sha(data), "mode": path.stat().st_mode & 0o777,
                    "storage": "gzip-v1", "stored_relative": str(stored_relative),
                    "stored_bytes": len(packed),
                })
            manifest[str(path)] = item
        _atomic(
            self.root / "manifest.json",
            (json.dumps(manifest, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, sort_keys=True) + "\n").encode(),
            0o600,
        )

    def restore(self):
        current = _matching_paths(self.codes)
        removed, restored = [], []
        for path in sorted(current - self.present, reverse=True):
            if path.is_file():
                path.unlink()
                removed.append(str(path))
        manifest = json.loads((self.root / "manifest.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        for path in sorted(self.present):
            item = manifest[str(path)]
            stored = (self.root / str(item["stored_relative"])).resolve()
            if self.root.resolve() not in stored.parents or not stored.is_file():
                raise PublishError("SNAPSHOT_GZIP_SCOPE:" + str(path))
            data = _ua099_gzip.decompress(_read(stored))
            if _sha(data) != item["sha256"]:
                raise PublishError("SNAPSHOT_GZIP_SHA:" + str(path))
            if not path.is_file() or _read(path) != data:
                _atomic(path, data, int(item.get("mode") or 0o644))
                restored.append(str(path))
        mismatches = [
            str(path) for path in self.present
            if not path.is_file() or _sha(_read(path)) != manifest[str(path)]["sha256"]
        ]
        if mismatches:
            raise PublishError("ROLLBACK_READBACK_MISMATCH:" + ",".join(mismatches))
        return {"backup_root": str(self.root), "removed": removed, "restored": restored}
# <<< UA099 BOUNDED GZIP SNAPSHOT V1


# UA-SITE-COUNTERS-123:START
from ua_site_counters import prepare_updates as _ua123_prepare_updates

_UA123_BASE_SNAPSHOT = Snapshot
_UA123_BASE_INSTALL = _install_catalog


class Snapshot(_UA123_BASE_SNAPSHOT):
    """Include homepage counters in the existing publication rollback."""
    def __init__(self, codes):
        super().__init__(codes)
        manifest_path = self.root / 'manifest.json'
        manifest = json.loads(manifest_path.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
        for folder in ROOTS:
            path = folder / 'index.html'
            if not path.is_file() or str(path) in manifest:
                continue
            data = _read(path)
            relative = pathlib.Path('files') / (str(path.relative_to(ROOT)) + '.gz')
            _atomic(self.root / relative, _ua099_gzip.compress(data, compresslevel=9, mtime=0), 0o600)
            manifest[str(path)] = {
                'exists': True, 'path': str(path), 'sha256': _sha(data),
                'mode': path.stat().st_mode & 0o777, 'storage': 'gzip-v1',
                'stored_relative': str(relative),
            }
            self.before_paths.add(path)
            self.present.add(path)
        _atomic(manifest_path, (json.dumps(manifest,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2,sort_keys=True)+'\n').encode(), 0o600)


def _install_catalog(source, target):
    # Both callers already hold the publisher lock and own Snapshot rollback.
    result = _UA123_BASE_INSTALL(source, target)
    updates, counts = _ua123_prepare_updates(ROOTS)
    for path, before, after in updates:
        if _read(path) != before:
            raise PublishError('COUNTER_SOURCE_CHANGED:' + str(path))
        if after != before:
            _atomic(path, after, path.stat().st_mode & 0o777)
        if _read(path) != after:
            raise PublishError('COUNTER_READBACK:' + str(path))
    result['site_counters'] = counts
    rows, _ = _row_map()
    for path_string, evidence in result.get('installed', {}).items():
        path = pathlib.Path(path_string)
        evidence['sha256'] = _sha(_read(path))
        evidence['audit'] = _validate_catalog(_read(path).decode('utf-8'), rows)
    return result
# UA-SITE-COUNTERS-123:END


# UA-ART-UA0022-REBUILD-SERIALIZATION-V1:GUARD:START
from publication_fence import publication_fence as _ua0022_publication_fence

def _exclusive_lock():
    # Same inode as legacy publishers; one registry makes nesting reentrant.
    return _ua0022_publication_fence(timeout=WAIT_SECONDS)
# UA-ART-UA0022-REBUILD-SERIALIZATION-V1:GUARD:END

# ===== master_card.py =====
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
# UA-MASTER-CARD-V2 — единое мастер-правило карточек UA ART.
# Один источник истины: фото (фасад + фокус), чат, дедуп видео, CTA диагностики,
# трекинг контейнера, тексты этапов. Никаких правок отдельных HTML.

import os, re, io, json, shutil, sqlite3, hashlib, subprocess, tempfile

METKA  = "MCF-V18"
DOM    = "/home/Carix"
VIDEO  = os.path.join(DOM, "video")
BAZA   = os.path.join(DOM, "crm.db")
FOKUS  = os.path.join(DOM, "foto_fokus.json")
FASAD  = os.path.join(DOM, "fasad.json")
TRACK  = os.path.join(DOM, "tracking.json")
DUBLI  = os.path.join(DOM, "video_dubli.json")
STAGE  = os.path.join(DOM, "video", "stage")
OPT    = os.path.join(DOM, "video", "opt")
CEL_W, CEL_H = 880, 815           # замер окна: 203x188, аспект 1.08

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════ CRM
def stage_po_statusu(status):
    s = (status or "").strip().lower()
    if s.startswith("ua_"):
        return "kiev"
    if s.startswith("ge_"):
        return "georgia"
    if s.startswith("sea_"):
        return "sea"
    if s.startswith("kr_"):
        return "korea"
    return ""

def vse_kody():
    """Список машин берётся ТОЛЬКО из CRM: 8, 9, 20 — сколько есть, столько и будет."""
    try:
        con = sqlite3.connect("file:%s?mode=ro" % BAZA, uri=True)
        cur = con.cursor()
        cur.execute("select auto_number from cars where auto_number is not null "
                    "order by auto_number")
        kody = [r[0] for r in cur.fetchall() if r[0]]
        con.close()
        return kody
    except Exception:
        return []

def kody_na_sayte():
    """Машины, у которых есть страница — тоже без жёсткого диапазона."""
    if not os.path.isdir(VIDEO):
        return []
    return sorted(set(re.findall(r"^(UA-\d{4})\.html$", "\n".join(os.listdir(VIDEO)),
                                 re.M)))

def dannye(kod):
    try:
        con = sqlite3.connect("file:%s?mode=ro" % BAZA, uri=True)
        con.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
        cur = con.cursor()
        cur.execute("select * from cars where auto_number=?", (kod,))
        r = cur.fetchone()
        con.close()
        return dict(r) if r else {}
    except Exception:
        return {}

def _json_fajl(put):
    try:
        with io.open(put, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            return json.load(f)
    except Exception:
        return {}

def _zapisat_json(put, d):
    try:
        vrem = put + ".tmp"
        with io.open(vrem, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write(json.dumps(d, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1))
        os.replace(vrem, put)
    except Exception:
        pass

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════ ТРЕКИНГ
LINII = [
    (("ONEY", "ONEU", "NYKU", "MOLU", "KKLU"), "ONE",
     "[URL]"),
]

def tracking(nomer):
    n = (nomer or "").strip().upper().replace(" ", "")
    if not n:
        return (None, None)
    ruchnoy = _json_fajl(TRACK)
    if n in ruchnoy and ruchnoy[n]:
        return ("CRM", ruchnoy[n])
    for prefiksy, imya, shablon in LINII:
        for pref in prefiksy:
            if n.startswith(pref):
                hvost = n[4:] if (imya == "ONE" and n.startswith("ONEY") and len(n) > 12) else n
                return (imya, shablon % hvost)
    return (None, None)

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════ ФОТО
def _razmer(put):
    try:
        from PIL import Image
        with Image.open(put) as im:
            return im.size
    except Exception:
        return (0, 0)

def fokus_fajla(put):
    """Точка фокуса: центр масс детализации (кузов детальный, небо и асфальт ровные)."""
    kesh = _json_fajl(FOKUS)
    klyuch = put.replace(DOM, "")
    try:
        metka = "%d" % int(os.path.getmtime(put))
    except Exception:
        metka = "0"
    if kesh.get(klyuch, {}).get("m") == metka:
        return (kesh[klyuch]["x"], kesh[klyuch]["y"])
    x, y = 50, 50
    try:
        from PIL import Image
        import numpy as np
        with Image.open(put) as im:
            im = im.convert("L")
            im.thumbnail((180, 180))
            a = np.asarray(im, dtype="float32")
        e = np.abs(np.diff(a, axis=1))[:-1, :] + np.abs(np.diff(a, axis=0))[:, :-1]
        m = (e > float(np.median(e)) * 1.6 + 1.0).astype("float32")
        if m.sum() > 20:
            h, w = m.shape
            ys, xs = np.nonzero(m)
            x = max(20, min(80, int(round(float(xs.mean()) / max(w - 1, 1) * 100))))
            y = max(25, min(75, int(round(float(ys.mean()) / max(h - 1, 1) * 100))))
    except Exception:
        pass
    kesh[klyuch] = {"x": x, "y": y, "m": metka}
    _zapisat_json(FOKUS, kesh)
    return (x, y)

def kandidat_fasada(kod):
    """ПОДСКАЗКА для владельца, а не решение: горизонтальный крупный кадр.
    Никогда не используется как «фасад» — перед от зада без модели не отличить."""
    papka = os.path.join(VIDEO, "foto", kod)
    if not os.path.isdir(papka):
        return ""
    luchshee, ball = "", -1.0
    for f in sorted(os.listdir(papka))[:16]:
        if f.startswith(".") or not f.lower().endswith((".jpg", ".jpeg", ".png", ".webp")):
            continue
        put = os.path.join(papka, f)
        w, h = _razmer(put)
        if not w or not h or float(w) / float(h) < 1.15:
            continue
        b = min(float(w * h) / 1200000.0, 1.5)
        try:
            b += min(os.path.getsize(put) / 250000.0, 1.0)
        except Exception:
            pass
        if b > ball:
            luchshee, ball = f, b
    return luchshee

def pervoe_foto(kod):
    """ЕДИНОЕ ПРАВИЛО: первое фото автомобиля и есть главное."""
    papka = os.path.join(VIDEO, "foto", kod)
    if not os.path.isdir(papka):
        return ""
    fajly = sorted([f for f in os.listdir(papka)
                    if not f.startswith(".")
                    and f.lower().endswith((".jpg", ".jpeg", ".png", ".webp"))])
    return fajly[0] if fajly else ""

def front_podtverzhden(kod, m=None):
    """Главное фото определено всегда: либо cover_photo из CRM, либо первое фото.
    Ручной выбор фасада не требуется."""
    return bool(pervoe_foto(kod)) or bool((m or dannye(kod)).get("cover_photo"))

def vybrat_glavnoe(kod, m=None):
    """1) cover_photo из CRM (если менеджер задал), 2) первое фото автомобиля.
    Правило одинаково для существующих и всех будущих карточек."""
    papka = os.path.join(VIDEO, "foto", kod)
    if not os.path.isdir(papka):
        return ("001.jpg", "папки фото нет")
    fajly = sorted([f for f in os.listdir(papka)
                    if not f.startswith(".")
                    and f.lower().endswith((".jpg", ".jpeg", ".png", ".webp"))])
    if not fajly:
        return ("001.jpg", "фотографий нет")
    if m is None:
        m = dannye(kod)
    from photo_visibility import visible_names
    fajly = visible_names(m if m is not None else dannye(kod), fajly)
    if not fajly:
        raise ValueError("No visible photo available for cover")
    cover = os.path.basename((m.get("cover_photo") or "").strip()) if m else ""
    if cover and cover in fajly:
        return (cover, "cover_photo из CRM")
    return (fajly[0], "первое фото автомобиля")

def zakrepit_pervoe_foto_v_crm(kody):
    """Пишет главное фото в cars.cover_photo, чтобы источник истины был один
    и повторная публикация его не меняла. Меняется только эта колонка."""
    sdelano = []
    try:
        con = sqlite3.connect(BAZA)
        cur = con.cursor()
        for kod in kody:
            imya = pervoe_foto(kod)
            if not imya:
                continue
            cur.execute("select cover_photo from cars where auto_number=?", (kod,))
            r = cur.fetchone()
            tek = os.path.basename((r[0] or "").strip()) if r and r[0] else ""
            if tek == imya:
                continue
            cur.execute("update cars set cover_photo=? where auto_number=?", (imya, kod))
            if cur.rowcount:
                sdelano.append("%s → %s" % (kod, imya))
        con.commit()
        con.close()
    except Exception as e:
        return ["ошибка записи: %s" % e]
    return sdelano

def zapisat_fasad_v_crm():
    """Переносит подтверждённые фасады из fasad.json в cars.cover_photo.
    Трогается ровно одна колонка, остальные данные не меняются."""
    fasady = _json_fajl(FASAD)
    if not fasady:
        return []
    sdelano = []
    try:
        con = sqlite3.connect(BAZA)
        cur = con.cursor()
        for kod, imya in fasady.items():
            imya = os.path.basename("%s" % imya)
            if not imya:
                continue
            cur.execute("update cars set cover_photo=? where auto_number=?", (imya, kod))
            if cur.rowcount:
                sdelano.append("%s → %s" % (kod, imya))
        con.commit()
        con.close()
    except Exception as e:
        return ["ошибка записи: %s" % e]
    return sdelano


# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════ SMART CROP: КАДР ПОД БЛОК
def bbox_obekta(put):
    """Прямоугольник, в котором лежит сам автомобиль (доли 0..1).
    Кузов детальный, небо и асфальт ровные — берём границы массы деталей."""
    try:
        from PIL import Image
        import numpy as np
        with Image.open(put) as im:
            im = im.convert("L")
            im.thumbnail((260, 260))
            a = np.asarray(im, dtype="float32")
    except Exception:
        return (0.0, 0.0, 1.0, 1.0)
    h, w = a.shape
    if w < 30 or h < 30:
        return (0.0, 0.0, 1.0, 1.0)
    e = np.abs(np.diff(a, axis=1))[:-1, :] + np.abs(np.diff(a, axis=0))[:, :-1]
    porog = float(np.median(e)) * 1.5 + 1.0
    m = (e > porog).astype("float32")
    if m.sum() < 30:
        return (0.0, 0.0, 1.0, 1.0)
    def granicy(proekciya, dolya=0.04):
        s = proekciya.cumsum()
        vsego = s[-1]
        if vsego <= 0:
            return (0.0, 1.0)
        import numpy as np
        n0 = int(np.searchsorted(s, vsego * dolya))
        n1 = int(np.searchsorted(s, vsego * (1 - dolya)))
        return (n0 / float(len(proekciya)), max(n1, n0 + 1) / float(len(proekciya)))
    x0, x1 = granicy(m.sum(axis=0))
    y0, y1 = granicy(m.sum(axis=1))
    return (x0, y0, x1, y1)

def _cvet_kraya(im, storona):
    """Средний цвет крайней полосы снимка — им заполняется свободное поле.
    Это не blur и не дорисовка: сплошной цвет фона того же кадра."""
    w, h = im.size
    polosa = im.crop((0, 0, w, max(int(h * 0.06), 1))) if storona == "verh" else \
             im.crop((0, h - max(int(h * 0.06), 1), w, h)) if storona == "niz" else \
             im.crop((0, 0, max(int(w * 0.06), 1), h)) if storona == "levo" else \
             im.crop((w - max(int(w * 0.06), 1), 0, w, h))
    malenkiy = polosa.resize((1, 1))
    return malenkiy.getpixel((0, 0))

def sdelat_vitrinnoe(kod, imya_fajla):
    """РЕЖИМ C — реальное кадрирование: из снимка вырезается окно точно в
    пропорции витринного блока, центрированное по автомобилю. Кадр заполняет
    окно целиком, срезается только фон. Ни заливки краёв, ни полей, ни blur."""
    ishod = os.path.join(VIDEO, "foto", kod, imya_fajla)
    if not os.path.exists(ishod):
        return ("", {"ok": False, "prichina": "файла нет"})
    try:
        os.makedirs(STAGE)
    except Exception:
        pass
    try:
        from PIL import Image
        with Image.open(ishod) as im:
            im = im.convert("RGB")
            w, h = im.size
            aspekt = float(CEL_W) / float(CEL_H)
            bx0, by0, bx1, by1 = bbox_obekta(ishod)
            px0, py0, px1, py1 = bx0 * w, by0 * h, bx1 * w, by1 * h
            # максимально возможное окно нужных пропорций внутри снимка
            if w / float(h) >= aspekt:
                ch = float(h)
                cw = ch * aspekt
            else:
                cw = float(w)
                ch = cw / aspekt
            cx, cy = (px0 + px1) / 2.0, (py0 + py1) / 2.0
            left = min(max(cx - cw / 2.0, 0.0), max(w - cw, 0.0))
            top = min(max(cy - ch / 2.0, 0.0), max(h - ch, 0.0))
            srezano = 1.0 - (cw * ch) / float(w * h)
            vidno_x = max(0.0, min(px1, left + cw) - max(px0, left))
            vidno_y = max(0.0, min(py1, top + ch) - max(py0, top))
            ploshad = max((px1 - px0) * (py1 - py0), 1.0)
            srez_kuzova = 1.0 - (vidno_x * vidno_y) / ploshad
            kadr = im.crop((int(left), int(top),
                            int(left + cw), int(top + ch))).resize(
                                (CEL_W, CEL_H), Image.LANCZOS)
            put_jpg = os.path.join(STAGE, "%s.jpg" % kod)
            put_webp = os.path.join(STAGE, "%s.webp" % kod)
            kadr.save(put_jpg, quality=86, optimize=True, progressive=True)
            try:
                kadr.save(put_webp, quality=84, method=5)
            except Exception:
                put_webp = ""
    except Exception as e:
        return ("", {"ok": False, "prichina": "%s" % e})
    legkiy = put_webp or put_jpg
    return ("/video/stage/%s" % os.path.basename(legkiy),
            {"ok": True, "celikom": srez_kuzova < 0.02, "bez_poley": True,
             "dolya_polya": 0, "srez": round(srezano * 100),
             "srez_kuzova": round(srez_kuzova * 100), "ishod": imya_fajla,
             "ves": os.path.getsize(legkiy), "razmer": "%dx%d" % (CEL_W, CEL_H)})

def podgotovit_vitrinu(kod, m=None):
    """ЖИВОЕ ФОТО = ПЕРВОЕ ФОТО АВТОМОБИЛЯ. Никакого перебора кадров,
    никакого «лучшего снимка»: только фото №1 из галереи машины
    (или cover_photo, если менеджер задал его в CRM)."""
    if m is None:
        m = dannye(kod)
    imya, istochnik = vybrat_glavnoe(kod, m)
    pervoe = pervoe_foto(kod)
    put, otchet = sdelat_vitrinnoe(kod, imya)
    otchet["foto"] = imya
    otchet["pervoe_foto"] = pervoe
    otchet["istochnik"] = istochnik
    otchet["sovpadaet_s_pervym"] = bool(imya and imya == pervoe)
    otchet["podtverzhden"] = True
    return (put, otchet)

def stil_fokusa(kod, imya_fajla, vitrina=False, vazhno=False):
    """Кадр витрины уже подготовлен точно под окно, поэтому здесь только
    заполнение окна без искажения пропорций."""
    vzh = "!important" if vazhno else ""
    if vitrina:
        return "object-fit:cover%s;object-position:50%% 50%%%s" % (vzh, vzh)
    put = os.path.join(VIDEO, "foto", kod, imya_fajla or "")
    x, y = fokus_fajla(put) if imya_fajla and os.path.exists(put) else (50, 50)
    return "object-position:%d%% %d%%%s" % (x, y, vzh)

def _pravka_tega_foto(teg, kod, glav, vitrina):
    src = re.search(r'src=["\']([^"\']*/foto/(UA-\d{4})/([^"\']+))["\']', teg)
    if not src:
        return teg
    tek = src.group(3)
    if glav and glav != tek:
        teg = teg.replace("/foto/%s/%s" % (kod, tek), "/foto/%s/%s" % (kod, glav))
    if "mcf-foto" in teg:
        return teg
    stil = stil_fokusa(kod, glav or tek, vitrina=vitrina, vazhno=vitrina)
    m = re.search(r'style=["\']([^"\']*)["\']', teg)
    if m:
        staryy = re.sub(r"object-(fit|position)\s*:[^;]*;?", "", m.group(1))
        teg = teg.replace(m.group(0), 'style="%s;%s" data-mcf-foto="1"'
                          % (staryy.rstrip(";"), stil), 1)
    else:
        teg = teg[:-1].rstrip("/") + ' style="%s" data-mcf-foto="1">' % stil
    return teg

def _dhash(put_kadra):
    try:
        from PIL import Image
        import numpy as np
        with Image.open(put_kadra) as im:
            im = im.convert("L").resize((9, 8))
            a = np.asarray(im, dtype="int16")
        bity = (a[:, 1:] > a[:, :-1]).flatten()
        v = 0
        for b in bity:
            v = (v << 1) | int(b)
        return v
    except Exception:
        return None

def _dlitelnost(put):
    try:
        r = subprocess.run(["ffprobe", "-v", "error", "-show_entries", "format=duration",
                            "-of", "default=nw=1:nk=1", put],
                           capture_output=True, timeout=25)
        return float((r.stdout or b"0").decode().strip() or 0)
    except Exception:
        return 0.0

def _put_iz_src(src):
    if src.startswith("/video/"):
        return os.path.join(DOM, src.lstrip("/"))
    return src if src.startswith("/") else os.path.join(VIDEO, src)

def _rasstoyanie(a, b):
    return bin(a ^ b).count("1")

def otpechatki_kadrov(put):
    """Пять кадров: начало, 25 %, 50 %, 75 %, конец — устойчиво к пережатию."""
    d = _dlitelnost(put)
    if d <= 0:
        return []
    rez = []
    papka = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="mcf_kadry_")
    try:
        for dolya in (0.05, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 0.95):
            kadr = os.path.join(papka, "k%d.jpg" % int(dolya * 100))
            try:
                subprocess.run(["ffmpeg", "-nostdin", "-v", "error", "-ss", "%.2f" % (d * dolya),
                                "-i", put, "-frames:v", "1", "-y", kadr],
                               capture_output=True, timeout=40)
            except Exception:
                continue
            if os.path.exists(kadr):
                h = _dhash(kadr)
                if h is not None:
                    rez.append(h)
    finally:
        try:
            for f in os.listdir(papka):
                os.remove(os.path.join(papka, f))
            os.rmdir(papka)
        except Exception:
            pass
    return rez

def otpechatok_video(put):
    h = hashlib.sha256()
    try:
        with io.open(put, "rb") as f:
            while True:
                b = f.read(524288)
                if not b:
                    break
                h.update(b)
        return h.hexdigest()
    except Exception:
        return None

def sodejatelno_odno(put1, put2):
    """True — практически один и тот же ролик (пережатый, обрезанный по краям и т. п.)."""
    if otpechatok_video(put1) == otpechatok_video(put2):
        return True
    k1, k2 = otpechatki_kadrov(put1), otpechatki_kadrov(put2)
    if len(k1) < 3 or len(k2) < 3:
        return False
    n = min(len(k1), len(k2))
    sovpalo = sum(1 for i in range(n) if _rasstoyanie(k1[i], k2[i]) <= 8)
    return sovpalo >= max(3, int(n * 0.6))

def ubrat_dubli_video(html, kod=""):
    """Уровень 1 — SHA256, уровень 2 — сравнение кадров. Файлы не удаляются:
    повтор просто не показывается, решение пишется в video_dubli.json."""
    bloki = list(re.finditer(r"<video\b[^>]*>.*?</video>|<video\b[^>]*/?>", html, re.S))
    if len(bloki) < 2:
        return html, 0
    zhurnal = _json_fajl(DUBLI)
    ostavleny, ubrano, rez, kon = [], 0, [], 0
    for m in bloki:
        src = re.search(r'src="([^"]+)"', m.group(0))
        if not src:
            continue
        put = _put_iz_src(src.group(1))
        if not os.path.exists(put):
            continue
        dubl = False
        for bylo, bylo_src in ostavleny:
            # ТОЛЬКО один и тот же файл, вставленный дважды (защита от
            # размножения при повторной публикации). Разные ролики не трогаем.
            if os.path.abspath(bylo) == os.path.abspath(put) or \
                    otpechatok_video(bylo) == otpechatok_video(put):
                dubl = True
                break
        if dubl:
            rez.append(html[kon:m.start()])
            kon = m.end()
            ubrano += 1
        else:
            ostavleny.append((put, src.group(1)))
    _zapisat_json(DUBLI, zhurnal)
    if ubrano:
        rez.append(html[kon:])
        return ("".join(rez), ubrano)
    return (html, 0)


def video_uzhe_est(kod, put_novogo):
    """Для точки загрузки видео в CRM: True → VIDEO ALREADY EXISTS."""
    for koren, _, fajly in os.walk(VIDEO):
        if "/preview" in koren or "/archive" in koren:
            continue
        for f in fajly:
            if f.startswith(".") or not f.lower().endswith((".mp4", ".mov", ".m4v", ".webm")):
                continue
            if kod not in f and kod not in koren:
                continue
            put = os.path.join(koren, f)
            if os.path.abspath(put) == os.path.abspath(put_novogo):
                continue
            if sodejatelno_odno(put, put_novogo):
                return True
    return False

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════ ОБЛЕГЧЕНИЕ КАРТИНОК
def oblegchit(src, max_w=900, kachestvo=82):
    """Делает лёгкую webp-копию тяжёлой картинки в /video/opt и возвращает её адрес.
    Оригиналы не трогаются. Если копия уже свежая — используется она."""
    if not src.startswith("/video/") or "/opt/" in src or "/stage/" in src:
        return src
    ishod = os.path.join(DOM, src.lstrip("/").split("?")[0])
    if not os.path.exists(ishod):
        return src
    try:
        if os.path.getsize(ishod) < 70000:
            return src               # и так лёгкая
    except Exception:
        return src
    imya = re.sub(r"[^A-Za-z0-9_.-]", "_", src.lstrip("/"))
    imya = os.path.splitext(imya)[0] + ".webp"
    cel = os.path.join(OPT, imya)
    try:
        if os.path.exists(cel) and os.path.getmtime(cel) >= os.path.getmtime(ishod):
            return "/video/opt/%s" % imya
    except Exception:
        pass
    try:
        os.makedirs(OPT)
    except Exception:
        pass
    try:
        from PIL import Image
        with Image.open(ishod) as im:
            im = im.convert("RGB")
            if im.size[0] > max_w:
                k = max_w / float(im.size[0])
                im = im.resize((max_w, max(int(im.size[1] * k), 1)), Image.LANCZOS)
            im.save(cel, quality=kachestvo, method=5)
    except Exception:
        return src
    try:
        if os.path.getsize(cel) >= os.path.getsize(ishod):
            return src               # выигрыша нет
    except Exception:
        pass
    return "/video/opt/%s" % imya

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════ КАНОНИЧЕСКИЕ ПУТИ
def kanonicheskiy_put(kod, papka=None):
    """Один production-путь на машину. Хеш — только метаданные версии."""
    return os.path.join(papka or VIDEO, "%s.html" % kod)

def versiya_kartochki(kod):
    put = kanonicheskiy_put(kod)
    try:
        with io.open(put, "rb") as f:
            return hashlib.sha256(f.read()).hexdigest()[:8]
    except Exception:
        return ""

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════ ДЕДУП ФАЙЛОВ МАШИНЫ
ARHIV_DUBLEY = os.path.join(DOM, "archive", "video_dubli")

def video_fajly_mashiny(kod):
    spisok = []
    for koren, _, fajly in os.walk(VIDEO):
        if "/preview" in koren or "/archive" in koren or "/stage" in koren:
            continue
        for f in sorted(fajly):
            if f.startswith(".") or not f.lower().endswith((".mp4", ".mov", ".m4v", ".webm")):
                continue
            if f.startswith(kod):
                spisok.append(os.path.join(koren, f))
    return spisok

def svedeniya_video(put):
    d = {"put": put, "razmer": 0, "dlitelnost": 0.0, "razreshenie": "?", "sha": ""}
    try:
        d["razmer"] = os.path.getsize(put)
    except Exception:
        pass
    d["sha"] = otpechatok_video(put) or ""
    d["dlitelnost"] = round(_dlitelnost(put), 2)
    try:
        r = subprocess.run(["ffprobe", "-v", "error", "-select_streams", "v:0",
                            "-show_entries", "stream=width,height",
                            "-of", "csv=p=0:s=x", put], capture_output=True, timeout=25)
        d["razreshenie"] = (r.stdout or b"").decode().strip() or "?"
    except Exception:
        pass
    return d

def ubrat_dubli_v_istochnike(kod):
    """Содержательные дубли ФАЙЛОВ машины уезжают в archive/video_dubli.
    Оригинал не уничтожается, генератор их больше не видит — значит и cikl2 тоже."""
    fajly = video_fajly_mashiny(kod)
    otchet = {"bylo": [svedeniya_video(f) for f in fajly], "ubrano": [], "shozhest": []}
    if len(fajly) < 2:
        otchet["stalo"] = len(fajly)
        return otchet
    try:
        os.makedirs(ARHIV_DUBLEY)
    except Exception:
        pass
    ostavleny = []
    for put in sorted(fajly, key=lambda f: -os.path.getsize(f)):
        dubl_dlya = None
        for bylo in ostavleny:
            if sodejatelno_odno(bylo, put):
                dubl_dlya = bylo
                break
        if dubl_dlya and False:
            k1, k2 = otpechatki_kadrov(dubl_dlya), otpechatki_kadrov(put)
            n = min(len(k1), len(k2))
            sovpalo = sum(1 for i in range(n) if _rasstoyanie(k1[i], k2[i]) <= 8)
            otchet["shozhest"].append("%s ≈ %s: совпало кадров %d из %d"
                                      % (os.path.basename(dubl_dlya),
                                         os.path.basename(put), sovpalo, n))
            cel = os.path.join(ARHIV_DUBLEY, os.path.basename(put))
            try:
                shutil.move(put, cel)
                otchet["ubrano"].append("%s → archive/video_dubli/" % os.path.basename(put))
            except Exception as e:
                otchet["ubrano"].append("не удалось убрать %s: %s" % (put, e))
        else:
            ostavleny.append(put)
    otchet["stalo"] = len(video_fajly_mashiny(kod))
    return otchet

def unikalnye_video(puti):
    """Сколько содержательно РАЗНЫХ роликов в списке файлов."""
    gruppy = []
    for put in puti:
        nashli = False
        for g in gruppy:
            if sodejatelno_odno(g[0], put):
                g.append(put)
                nashli = True
                break
        if not nashli:
            gruppy.append([put])
    return gruppy

def svesti_k_unikalnym(kod):
    """ХУК ДЕДУПА: вызывается публикацией ДО сборки HTML. Копии одного и того же
    материала уводятся в archive/video_dubli, разные ролики остаются."""
    fajly = video_fajly_mashiny(kod)
    if len(fajly) < 2:
        return {"fajlov": len(fajly), "unikalnyh": len(fajly), "ubrano": []}
    gruppy = unikalnye_video(fajly)
    ubrano = []
    arhiv = os.path.join(DOM, "archive", "video_dubli")
    for g in gruppy:
        for lishniy in g[1:]:
            try:
                os.makedirs(arhiv)
            except Exception:
                pass
            try:
                shutil.move(lishniy, os.path.join(arhiv, os.path.basename(lishniy)))
                ubrano.append(os.path.basename(lishniy))
            except Exception:
                pass
    ostalos = video_fajly_mashiny(kod)
    return {"fajlov": len(ostalos), "unikalnyh": len(unikalnye_video(ostalos)),
            "ubrano": ubrano}

def nayti_poteryannoe_video(kod, imeyushiesya):
    """Ищет в uploads, archive и резервах ролик машины, которого нет среди текущих."""
    mesta = [os.path.join(DOM, "uploads"), os.path.join(DOM, "archive")]
    kandidaty = []
    for mesto in mesta:
        if not os.path.isdir(mesto):
            continue
        for koren, _, fajly in os.walk(mesto):
            for f in fajly:
                if f.startswith(".") or not f.lower().endswith((".mp4", ".mov", ".m4v", ".webm")):
                    continue
                if kod not in f:
                    continue
                kandidaty.append(os.path.join(koren, f))
    novye = []
    for k in kandidaty:
        if any(sodejatelno_odno(k, e) for e in imeyushiesya + novye):
            continue
        novye.append(k)
    return novye

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════ ЧАТ (БЕЗ ВСТАВКИ HTML В ТЕКСТ)

CHAT_CSS = """<style id="mcf-chat-css">
/* Desktop не меняем */
a.mcf-chat,.mcf-chat{position:fixed!important;right:16px!important;left:auto!important;
bottom:calc(16px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom))!important;z-index:9990!important;

width:auto!important;padding:11px 16px!important;border-radius:999px!important;
font-size:15px!important;line-height:1!important;white-space:nowrap!important}
/* Телефон: маленький кружок, не закрывает автомобиль, стрелку и текст.
   Правило ловит кнопку по ссылке на WhatsApp и слову chat в классе,
   поэтому работает даже если наш класс не проставился. */
@media (max-width:900px){

flex:0 0 auto!important;display:block!important}

body a[href*="wa.me"][class*="chat"] > *:not(svg){display:none!important}
body a.mcf-chat::after,body a[href*="wa.me"][class*="chat"]::after{content:""!important}
}
</style>"""

MARKERY_STARYE = ("mcf-ch-full", "mcf-ch-short")
_OTKR = ("<", "&lt;", "\\u003c", "\u003c", "&#60;", "&#x3c;", "&#x3C;")
_ZAKR = (">", "&gt;", "\\u003e", "\u003e", "&#62;", "&#x3e;", "&#x3E;")

def _granica_vlevo(html, poz):
    luchshe, dlina = -1, 0
    for o in _OTKR:
        i = html.rfind(o, max(0, poz - 400), poz)
        if i > luchshe:
            luchshe, dlina = i, len(o)
    return luchshe

def _granica_vpravo(html, poz):
    luchshe, dlina = len(html), 0
    for z in _ZAKR:
        i = html.find(z, poz, poz + 400)
        if i != -1 and i < luchshe:
            luchshe, dlina = i, len(z)
    return luchshe + dlina if dlina else -1

def fragment(html, vokrug="Чат online", shirina=130):
    """Кусок страницы вокруг надписи кнопки — чтобы видеть реальный вид."""
    poz = html.find(vokrug)
    if poz < 0:
        for mk in MARKERY_STARYE:
            poz = html.find(mk)
            if poz >= 0:
                break
    if poz < 0:
        return ""
    return html[max(0, poz - shirina):poz + shirina].replace("\n", " ")

def ochistit_staroe(html):
    """Вырезает вставки прежней версии по МАРКЕРУ, а не по форме записи:
    границы тега ищутся во всех видах (< , &lt; , \\u003c и т. п.),
    поэтому работает и в обычном HTML, и в экранированном, и внутри JS-строки."""
    if not any(mk in html for mk in MARKERY_STARYE):
        return html
    for _ in range(60):
        poz = -1
        for mk in MARKERY_STARYE:
            i = html.find(mk)
            if i >= 0 and (poz < 0 or i < poz):
                poz = i
        if poz < 0:
            break
        a = _granica_vlevo(html, poz)
        b = _granica_vpravo(html, poz)
        if a < 0 or b <= a:
            html = html.replace(html[poz:poz + len("mcf-ch-full")], "", 1)
            continue
        kusok = html[a:b]
        if (b - a) > 140 or "<a " in kusok or "href=" in kusok:
            # опасно: можно снести саму ссылку — убираем только сам маркер
            html = html[:poz] + html[poz + len("mcf-ch-full"):] \
                if html[poz:poz + len("mcf-ch-full")] == "mcf-ch-full" \
                else html[:poz] + html[poz + len("mcf-ch-short"):]
            continue
        html = html[:a] + html[b:]
    # осиротевшие закрывающие теги рядом с надписью кнопки
    poz = html.find("Чат online")
    if poz >= 0:
        n, k = max(0, poz - 400), min(len(html), poz + 400)
        okno = html[n:k]
        for zakr in ("&lt;/span&gt;", "\\u003c/span\\u003e", "\u003c/span\u003e",
                     "&#60;/span&#62;", "</span>"):
            okno = okno.replace(zakr, "")
        okno = okno.replace("Чат onlineЧат", "Чат online").replace("Чат online Чат", "Чат online")
        html = html[:n] + okno + html[k:]
    return html

CHAT_TELEFON = "[N]"
_CHAT_SVG = ('<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="currentColor" aria-hidden="true">'
             '<path d="M12 2a[N].7 15l-1.2 4.4 4.5-1.2A[N]zm5.6 '
             '14.2c-.2.6-1.2 1.2-1.7 1.2-.4 0-1 .1-3.2-.8-2.7-1.1-4.4-3.9-4.5-4-.1-.2-1-1.4-1-2.7 '
             '0-1.3.7-1.9.9-2.2.2-.2.5-.3.7-.3h.5c.2 0 .4 0 .6.5l.8 2c.1.2.1.4 0 .5l-.3.5-.4.4c-.1.1-.3.3-.1.6.2.3.8 '
             '1.3 1.7 2.1 1.2 1 2.1 1.3 2.4 1.5.3.1.5.1.6-.1l.9-1c.2-.2.4-.2.6-.1l1.9.9c.2.1.4.2.5.3.1.2.1.7-.1 1.2z"/></svg>')
CHAT_VIDZHET = ('<a class="mcf-chat" href="[URL]" target="_blank" rel="noopener" '
                'aria-label="Написать в WhatsApp" '
                'style="background:#25D366;color:#08210f;text-decoration:none;font-weight:700">'
                '%s<span>Чат online</span></a>' % (CHAT_TELEFON, _CHAT_SVG))

def _skolko_chatov(html):
    return html.count('class="mcf-chat')

def obespechit_chat(html):
    """ПОРЯДОК: убрать legacy → пометить существующий (лишние удалить) →
    если чата не осталось, вставить чистый мастер-виджет → добавить CSS.
    Валидация count = 1 делается только ПОСЛЕ этого."""
    html = ochistit_staroe(html)
    najdeno = [0]

    def zamena(m):
        otkr, vnutr, zakr = m.group(1), m.group(2), m.group(3)
        najdeno[0] += 1
        if najdeno[0] > 1:
            return ""
        if "mcf-chat" in otkr:
            return otkr + vnutr + zakr
        if 'class="' in otkr:
            otkr = otkr.replace('class="', 'class="mcf-chat ', 1)
        elif "class='" in otkr:
            otkr = otkr.replace("class='", "class='mcf-chat ", 1)
        else:
            otkr = otkr[:-1] + ' class="mcf-chat">'
        return otkr + vnutr + zakr

    html = re.sub(r"(<a\b[^>]*>)((?:(?!</a>).)*?Чат online(?:(?!</a>).)*?)(</a>)",
                  zamena, html, flags=re.S)
    if _skolko_chatov(html) == 0:
        # запасной поиск: плавающая ссылка на WhatsApp со словом «Чат»
        html = re.sub(r"(<a\b[^>]*wa\.me[^>]*>)((?:(?!</a>).)*?Чат(?:(?!</a>).)*?)(</a>)",
                      zamena, html, flags=re.S)
    if _skolko_chatov(html) == 0:
        if "</body>" in html:
            html = html.replace("</body>", CHAT_VIDZHET + "</body>", 1)
        else:
            html = html + CHAT_VIDZHET
    if 'id="mcf-chat-css"' not in html:
        html = html.replace("</head>", CHAT_CSS + "</head>", 1) \
            if "</head>" in html else CHAT_CSS + html
    if 'id="mcf-izmer"' not in html and "stage-card" in html:
        html = html.replace("</body>", IZMERITEL + "</body>", 1) \
            if "</body>" in html else html + IZMERITEL
    if 'id="mcf-stage-css"' not in html and "stage-card" in html:
        html = html.replace("</head>", STAGE_CSS + "</head>", 1) \
            if "</head>" in html else STAGE_CSS + html
    return html, _skolko_chatov(html)

def pochinit_chat(html):
    """Ровно один компактный чат. Только класс и CSS — разметка текста не трогается."""
    html = ochistit_staroe(html)
    najdeno = [0]

    def zamena(m):
        otkr, vnutr, zakr = m.group(1), m.group(2), m.group(3)
        najdeno[0] += 1
        if najdeno[0] > 1:
            return ""
        if "mcf-chat" in otkr:
            return otkr + vnutr + zakr
        if 'class="' in otkr:
            otkr = otkr.replace('class="', 'class="mcf-chat ', 1)
        elif "class='" in otkr:
            otkr = otkr.replace("class='", "class='mcf-chat ", 1)
        else:
            otkr = otkr[:-1] + ' class="mcf-chat">'
        return otkr + vnutr + zakr

    novyy = re.sub(r"(<a\b[^>]*>)((?:(?!</a>).)*?Чат online(?:(?!</a>).)*?)(</a>)",
                   zamena, html, flags=re.S)
    if najdeno[0] and 'id="mcf-chat-css"' not in novyy:
        novyy = novyy.replace("</head>", CHAT_CSS + "</head>", 1) \
            if "</head>" in novyy else CHAT_CSS + novyy
    return novyy, najdeno[0]

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════ ДИАГНОСТИКА: ПОНЯТНАЯ КНОПКА
def est_materialy(kod, m=None):
    """Активная кнопка только при реальных материалах именно этой машины."""
    if m is None:
        m = dannye(kod)
    if not os.path.exists(os.path.join(VIDEO, "%s-diag.html" % kod)):
        return False
    for pole in ("diag_link", "diag_text", "diag_report"):
        if ("%s" % (m.get(pole) or "")).strip():
            return True
    for pole in ("condition_photos", "condition_videos"):
        v = ("%s" % (m.get(pole) or "")).strip()
        if v and v not in ("[]", "null", "{}"):
            return True
    papka = os.path.join(VIDEO, "diag", kod)
    if os.path.isdir(papka):
        for f in os.listdir(papka):
            if not f.startswith(".") and f.lower().endswith(
                    (".jpg", ".jpeg", ".png", ".webp", ".mp4", ".mov", ".webm")):
                return True
    return False

_CTA_ON = (
    '<a class="mcf-diag-cta" href="{KOD}-diag.html" '
    'style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;margin:14px 0;padding:15px 16px;'
    'border-radius:14px;text-decoration:none;'
    'background:linear-gradient(180deg,rgba(212,175,55,.20),rgba(212,175,55,.08));'
    'border:1px solid rgba(212,175,55,.55);color:#f4e3ae">'
    '<span style="font-size:22px;line-height:1">🔧</span>'
    '<span style="flex:1">'
    '<span style="display:block;font-weight:800;font-size:16px">Комплексная диагностика →</span>'
    '<span style="display:block;font-size:13px;opacity:.85;margin-top:3px">'
    'ЛКП · OBD · ходовая · фото · видео</span></span>'
    '<span style="font-size:20px;opacity:.8">›</span></a>')

_CTA_OFF = (
    '<div class="mcf-diag-cta mcf-diag-off" '
    'style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;margin:14px 0;padding:15px 16px;'
    'border-radius:14px;background:rgba(255,255,255,.05);'
    'border:1px dashed rgba(255,255,255,.25);color:#c9d4e2">'
    '<span style="font-size:22px;line-height:1">🔧</span>'
    '<span style="flex:1"><span style="display:block;font-weight:700;font-size:15px">'
    'Диагностика готовится</span>'
    '<span style="display:block;font-size:13px;opacity:.8;margin-top:3px">'
    'Материалы проверки появятся здесь</span></span></div>')

def cta_diagnostiki(kod, m=None):
    return _CTA_ON.replace("{KOD}", kod) if est_materialy(kod, m) else _CTA_OFF

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════ ТЕКСТЫ ЭТАПОВ
